| CON-070 | Bug | Queued | Medium | Class follow-up of CON-030 (parent: CON-030; review r1 F5 and review r2 G9, section 11.4.276(C)): tools outside the full-suite runner parse human-oriented git output by whitespace or by lines where a field is a submodule name or path, so a name or path with a space (and, for (7), a leading or trailing space, a CR-class character or a newline) is truncated, merged or not found. Members, each with its measured effect (2026-11-05, fixtures with submodules "submodules/my engine" and "submodules/plain"; control: the plain path is read correctly every time): (1) progressive/validate_submodule.py:60 `l.split()[1]` on `-` status lines: with a crafted --status-file an orphan path "x y" and a declared path "x" gives PASS (false PASS, measured by the r1 reviewer; control "zz" FAILs); with real git output a spaced uninitialised path gives a false FAIL. (2) scripts/gates/cm_hub_engine_no_ownorg_nesting.sh:68 `--get-regexp` plus an awk match on field 2: drops "submodules/my engine" from the inspected set (measured by the r1 reviewer), so that engine is never inspected: potential false PASS, end to end UNCONFIRMED. (3) scripts/gates/cm_gitlink_on_remote.sh:55 and :57 `ls-tree` / `ls-files -s` plus awk field 4 as the path: truncated at the first space (UNCONFIRMED end to end); :63 `--get-regexp` plus an awk equality on field 2 returns no key for "submodules/my engine" (measured). (4) scripts/fastcycle/cycle/baseline_replay.sh:1049-1052 `IFS=' ' read -r key path` over `--get-regexp` output: measured name=[submodules/my] path=[engine.path submodules/my engine] for the spaced submodule, plain one correct; effect on the replay (wrong modules/<name> reference) UNCONFIRMED. (5) scripts/gates/cm_helix_deps_ref_matches_gitlink.sh:79, :95, :105 `--get-regexp` plus an awk equality on field 2: measured key=[] for "submodules/my engine" (key found for the plain path), so line 79 reports a false DRIFT with url <none> (fail closed); end to end UNCONFIRMED. (6) low: scripts/fastcycle/consumers/migrate.sh:2432-2433 and docs/prompts/setup/latest.md:290 compare against fixed constitution paths. (7) scripts/deps_manifest/helix_deps_manifest.py:318-325 (review r2 G9): reads .gitmodules with `git config --get-regexp` text, `splitlines()`, the regex `^submodule\.(.*)\.(path\|url) (.*)$` and `.strip()`, so a path with a leading or trailing space, a CR-class character or a newline is read wrong (by reading; not measured end to end, UNCONFIRMED). Fix shape proven in CON-030: NUL-separated machine formats (git config --null, git ls-files -s -z, git ls-tree -z), bytes decoded with os.fsdecode (surrogateescape), never whitespace or line splitting of a path. Each member needs its own RED on a spaced-path fixture first (section 11.4.292). | CON-144 | Bug | Ready for testing | medium | Found at the fixu landing (2026-11-10). On a clean worktree of main 90efceb8, bash scripts/gates/cm_gate_env_reads_allowlisted.sh exits 1 with uncovered=3: scripts/gates/cm_github_issues_source_anchor_wired.sh:334 <eval> (fixed by the fixu vehicle, CON-121), scripts/gates/cm_pr_delivery_anchor_wired.sh:130 BLK (read-before-assign) and scripts/gates/cm_release_gate_anchor_wired.sh:118 BLK (read-before-assign); on the merged tree the first one is gone and the other two remain (uncovered=2). Both lines are the names_cm_gate_in_block() helper, added by c87745de (section 11.4.297) and 2d783401 (section 11.4.298), which reads $BLK inside a function defined before BLK is assigned. Whether the read can happen before the assignment, or the census reports a safe read, is UNCONFIRMED (not investigated at the landing; out of the landing scope, section 11.4.293). Fix: route BLK through cm_gate_env_set, or add allowlist rows with a reason, after reading when the helper runs. Evidence: docs/qa/fixu_review/landing/envgate_main_90efceb8.out (main) and docs/qa/fixu_review/landing/envgate_merged.out (merged tree). Fix (2026-11-11, CON-144 fix vehicle, commit 77553d02 on main 27dcf518, merged with main 64ac151d in c41cf08f): reproduced first on clean 27dcf518, 9cd7cacd and 64ac151d (rc 1, uncovered=2, byte-identical output). Root cause established: the census judges text order; names_cm_gate_in_block() is defined above the top-level BLK= assignment, so its body read of $BLK is reported read-before-assign although its only call runs after the assignment. Decisive experiments: an ambient BLK=/nonexistent leaves both gates' output byte-identical (rc 0); moving only the definition below BLK= removes the census row. The UNCONFIRMED question above is answered: the read cannot happen before the assignment; the census row is a text-order report, not an ambient read. Fix: the helper takes the block file as its second argument and the call site passes "$BLK". Guard tests/gates/test_con144_blk_read_order.sh (needles N0/N1, C1..C5, paired mutants M1..M3): RED 7 FAIL with the fix stashed, GREEN 15/0; env gate PASS uncovered=0 stale=0 on the merged tree; the two anchor gates' mutation tests and the env gate's mutation test PASS. Evidence: docs/qa/con144_reproduction_2026_11_11.txt. Not done: full suite; independent review pending. **Affected scope / file-scope manifest:** scripts/gates/cm_pr_delivery_anchor_wired.sh; scripts/gates/cm_release_gate_anchor_wired.sh; tests/gates/test_con144_blk_read_order.sh; config/full_suite_manifest.tsv; docs/guides/full_suite_runner_guide.md **Verification:** provisional (section 11.4.290) -- job: re-run scripts/gates/cm_gate_env_reads_allowlisted.sh (expect PASS uncovered=0) and the guard tests/gates/test_con144_blk_read_order.sh (expect 15 passed, 0 failed) on the final main -- checks: census no longer reports BLK read-before-assign for the two anchor-wired gates; guard mutants M1..M3 fail -- layer: host-only (class floor: runtime for a gate item) -- target: host, repo checkout -- artifact: the commit that lands the CON-144 vehicle (9a4ea101 plus the tracker commit) -- due: 2026-11-23 (UNCONFIRMED: date set by the CON-PENDING-rehearsal r2 fixer when the checker began to require a date; the line said "next full-suite run"; no scheduler entry exists). Independent Opus review GO-with-follow-ups (0 critical, 0 important, 1 minor, 1 nit); follow-ups CON-192 (F1) and CON-193 (F3). Not VERIFIED-DONE. | CON-160 | Task | Ready for testing | Low | Review finding m2 (minor, test-instrumentation, parent CON-146). EP2V checks only that the refusal message names index (staged) content. Mutant Y12 (rc==1 returns None instead of True) and mutant Y14 (final return None becomes False, a git failure reads as no index content) both survive r22 (90/0). The verdict stays REFUSED either way, so this is message precision, not safety. Fix: assert index_unpatched=True in EP2V, and add a git-failure fixture (a PATH shim making git diff --quiet ^1 ^2 return 128) asserting index_unpatched=None; both must kill Y12 and Y14. | CON-216 | Bug | Ready for testing | Low | Parent: CON-161. Review finding m1 (minor, test-instrumentation). The r2 guard test_host_report_probe_thread_headroom_r2_regression.sh reaches thr None only through FC_GUARD_THREADS=xyz (the int() path), never through the ps-failure path (rc != 0 or out is None) of _read_ulimit_threads in host_report.py. Reviewer mutant RD (a failed ps reads as 0 threads, so headroom becomes the whole soft limit) survives r2 6/0, host_report r1 45/0 and test_host_limits_red 140/0. Fix: a case with FC_GUARD_THREADS unset and a PATH shim ps (or sh) that exits non-zero, asserting reason=unmeasured; RD as its paired mutant. Follow-up of the independent Opus review of the fu_a vehicle (CON-159..CON-162; verdict GO-with-follow-ups, 0 critical, 0 important, 3 minor, 4 nit; record docs/qa/review_fu_a_2026_11_11.md). Registered under section 11.4.276(G.2) for the next iteration. Status at landing: Ready for testing; a fix is claimed and is PROVISIONAL-DONE (section 11.4.290) until its own verification job passes; not VERIFIED-DONE.  Fix note (fix216, 2026-11-11): the review called this test-instrumentation, but the live reproduction showed a source defect. In _read_ulimit_threads of scripts/fastcycle/host/host_report.py the thread count came from sh -c 'ps ... 2>/dev/null \| wc -l', and the exit status of that pipeline is the one of wc, so a ps that fails read as "0" threads: with FC_GUARD_THREADS unset and a PATH shim ps that exits 3 the real function returned (1000, 0) and probe_thread_headroom went on with thread_headroom=1000 (the whole soft limit), exactly the behaviour of reviewer mutant RD. ps now runs directly (ps -L --no-headers -U <real uid>) and its lines are counted in Python, so a failed or unrunnable ps leaves the count None and the probe reports reason=unmeasured. Note: the count no longer includes the sh and wc processes of the old pipeline. Test: test_host_report_probe_thread_headroom_r2_regression.sh gains case PS_FAILS (FC_GUARD_THREADS unset, shim ps first on PATH, expect reason=unmeasured) and adopts RD verbatim as paired mutant RD_failed_ps_reads_zero (scoped to _read_ulimit_threads). RED: PS_FAILS failed on the pre-fix source (6 pass / 1 fail). GREEN: 7 pass / 0 fail under bash and sh, RD killed; r1 regression 45/0; test_host_limits_red SKIPs outside a consumer layout (as before). The L29 SKIP-with-reason of scripts/fastcycle/tests/test_consumer_required_helper.sh is honest in wording (closed reason topology_unsupported, not passed through ok()), but it does not reach the suite verdict: the run still ends SUMMARY: N pass / 0 fail with exit 0 and no skip count, while the same script exits 77 when dash is absent. | CON-223 | Task | Ready for testing | Low | Parent: CON-185. Review finding F2 (minor, test-instrumentation). FC_L29_CANDIDATES is test-only (it lives in the test file; the production helper is untouched), but it is read unconditionally from the ambient environment: an unquoted ${FC_L29_CANDIDATES:-...} means a whitespace or bogus value (" " expands to empty) turns a real host's L29 into a SKIP with rc 0, and nothing says an override was in effect. With CON-185 F1 (the SKIP not reaching the verdict) this is a silent weakening switch for the test. Fix: print a note such as 'note: FC_L29_CANDIDATES override in effect' (with its value) whenever the variable is set, and make an override-driven SKIP non-zero per the F1 follow-up. Fix: the stderr of every network command (git fetch, push, ls-remote, curl) is never printed on any path; a failure prints its exit status and one word of a closed set (rc_err_class, an awk program that prints string literals only) or timeout. rc_redact_cut stays as defence in depth for local stderr and the publisher's own lines, with J1 (rule order R3, R4, R1, R2, R5, R6) and J2 (no scan window, whole-line rules, lines over 16384 bytes not shown) fixed. Every caller and server value that is printed is validated first and never echoed when invalid (tag, SHA, remote names, --release specs, unknown arguments, --base, --prev-tag, notes path, base URL; ls-remote ids through oid(), the GitHub id, the HTTP status). J4: the publisher token goes to curl -K - on stdin, never a file, never argv, never exported (the round-4 TOKEN= kept the export attribute of a caller's TOKEN, so a child process received it: publisher case 19 was RED on the round-4 code). J5: every network command runs under timeout --foreground -k 10 RELEASE_NET_TIMEOUT (default 600 s) plus git http.lowSpeedLimit, curl -m 60 --connect-timeout 20. J6: the trap probe is a committed script (probe_trap_exit_status.py); round 4's no-observable-difference claim for mutants Q12 and S11 was wrong (exit 143/129 with the trap, killed by the signal without it), they are ordinary mutants now. J7: incident_j7.md (unscoped pkill of the round-4 session, severity UNKNOWN). Class census with control needles: census_print_sites.py, census_red_head.txt (178 sites), census_green.txt (195 sites); static case C40 fails if any captured network output variable is printed. RED/GREEN and the threat model: docs/qa/con227_234_2026_11_11/round5/. Mutation run: docs/qa/con227_234_2026_11_11/round5/mutations.txt. The round-5 census looked at printed text only; the review found three defects on other channels. K2: timeout --foreground signalled only its direct child, git's transport helpers ran on for 60-300 s against a hanging server (the test double, exec sleep 30, had no grandchild); --foreground is dropped, timeout signals the whole process group, C41 drives real git and git-remote-http(s) against a hanging local listener (exit 1, class word timeout, no git process left). K1: a remote URL with a password (or any userinfo on http/https), also after an insteadOf rewrite, is in the argv of git-remote-https and readable in /proc by every local user; the tag script now reads every fetch and push URL and aborts before any network command without showing the URL (rc_url_has_userinfo, C42). K3: the token and every exported caller variable reached git, timeout, gh and glab; rc_env_lockdown un-exports everything not on an allow-list and every exported function, the token is exported only to the publisher's bash (C43, C44, publisher 25, derive K3). K4: rc_safe_url is the base-URL check of the publisher. N1: three more awk backup shapes, noted as unreachable for network output. Structural: a channel census over every external command invocation (argv, environment, stdin, cwd, files, signals and time bound): tests/release/channels.tsv (one row per invocation, closed vocabulary), the control-needled static instrument tests/release/channel_census.py with a lint (missing or stale row, value outside the vocabulary, secret channel on the wrong row, unlisted secret reference, external command before rc_env_lockdown), and tests/release/test_channel_census.sh (instrument needles, lint on mutated copies, runtime recorders generated from the table). The runtime census found K5: dirname (library load) and date (derive script) ran before rc_env_lockdown and received the caller's token; fixed (parameter expansion, date after the lockdown) and refused by the lint. K6 (found adopting mutant X11): --token-var may name a variable on the allow-list (GH_HOST), which the lockdown leaves exported; the publisher dropped it only after its first grep; it now reads and unsets it before any external command (publisher case 28, every table command recorded). The tag, publisher and derive suites run the census lint on the script under test (case L1), so an external command before the lockdown is killable in the harness (Y22..Y25); census part 3 records every command the table names or the instrument finds. RELEASE_ENV_KEEP refuses any name with TOK (the publisher's _rpg_tok). A sixth instrument blind spot (a command after a case-arm pattern) was fixed and needled. Reviewer mutants X1..X20 adopted (X12 recorded equivalent under the lockdown, X12b killed), fixer mutants Y1..Y25 added; mp lines count a kill only by a FAIL line that names the property. Threat model, RED/GREEN, census and mutation run: docs/qa/con227_234_2026_11_11/round6/. Not independently re-reviewed after round 6. Round 7 (structural, review scratchpad/review_fix227_r6.md NO-GO on L1..L7, Z8, Z9): the allow-list of round 6 was subtractive and worked by name, so an inherited allexport re-exported the publisher's token (L1) and environment entries whose names are not shell identifiers reached every child (L2). rc_env_clean (scripts/release/redact_cut.sh), the first action of the three scripts, re-executes the script with exec -c (empty environment, no caller shell option) and rebuilds the allow-listed and the read variables from NUL-separated records on a pipe; clean state = marker argv0 + empty /proc/$$/environ, unreadable /proc exits 3, reserved or invalid name exits 2. Runtime census tests/release/env_census.py (recorders read their own /proc/$$/environ; names only) checks every child under a dirty environment, SHELLOPTS=allexport and bash -a (tag C48, publisher 29, derive L2). C49 kills Z8, Z9; suites skip HUP cases under an ignored SIGHUP with a control, the harness reports INCONCLUSIVE (exit 3); suite and harness signal handlers exit; L6 CHANGELOG ids corrected; L7 is CON-310, the trap shape outside tests/release is CON-311. Z1..Z14 adopted (no Z10, Z6 equivalent), W1..W13 added. Record: docs/qa/con227_234_2026_11_11/round7/. Not independently re-reviewed after round 7; still In progress. | CON-290 | Bug | Queued | Medium | **What:** the full-suite runner's tree-changed check misses an ignored file written inside a submodule. tree_state() in scripts/full_suite/run_full_suite.py lists and hashes ignored files with `git ls-files -o -i --exclude-standard -z` in the ROOT only; the `subs` record holds each submodule's status and diff, never its ignored files. A suite that writes an ignored file inside a submodule during the run therefore ends `RESULT: PASS-FULL` (rc 0), while the same write at the root (ignored by the root) ends `TREE CHANGED ... RESULT: FAIL` (rc 1). The same gap means a change of a submodule's ignored files does not change the resume key.  **Found by:** independent review of CON-030 round 3 (finding H4, pre-existing on main efe2cc6; outside CON-030's encoding/framing class, so registered as its own item per section 11.4.276(E)(2)). | CON-291 | Bug | Queued | Low | **What:** found by the CON-030 round 4 class sweep (docs/qa/con030_review/con030_class_sweep_r4.py, shape B sites at tree_ident: the 'subs' hash of 'git submodule status --recursive' and the dirty-list hash of 'git status --porcelain=v1'). Both outputs are split with str.splitlines() (which also splits on NEL, LS, PS, CR, VT, FF) and joined with LF before hashing. With core.quotePath=false a path holding U+0085 is printed raw, so one untracked path 'a<NEL>?? b' and the two untracked paths 'a' and 'b' give the same dirty fragments and the same dirty hash. Parent: CON-030. **Why no verdict effect (reasoned from the code, not measured):** a dirty tree at the start already ends RESULT: PARTIAL-DIRTY-TREE rc 10, never PASS-FULL, and content changes during the run are caught by tree_state (TREE CHANGED, rc 1) and by the structured quick identity; so the collision changes only which identity message is shown. With the default core.quotePath=true git quotes these bytes. **Acceptance:** read both outputs NUL-terminated (git status -z, and the submodule list from git ls-files -s -z as quick_ident already does) or frame each line with hrec(); a test with core.quotePath=false and a NEL path shows two different dirty hashes; a paired mutation restoring splitlines() is killed. **UNKNOWN:** whether any consumer sets core.quotePath=false.  **Type (review r4 K11):** retyped from Task to Bug: an identity hash that two different trees can share is a defect, even when no verdict effect was found.  **Id history (section 11.4.214):** first minted on vehicle/con030 as CON-109 (commit a14824bd); re-minted as CON-117 in CON-030 round 5 (vehicle commit ede6686e, review r4 K6), because live main 90efceb8 already owns CON-109 for another item, and again as CON-147 after the merge of live main 2e8cfb90, which owns CON-117 for another item. A run with a different value therefore resumes an earlier verdict.  **Found by:** independent review of CON-030 round 4 (finding K4, outside CON-030's declared class; pre-existing on main 90efceb8), registered as its own item per section 11.4.276(E)(2). A 3 s unit recorded with --timeout 60 is resumed by --timeout 1 (PASS-FULL rc 0; fresh control TIMEOUT rc 1). Drivers: drv/envextra_resume.sh and drv/timeout_resume.sh of the round-4 review (docs/qa/con030_review/review_con030_r4.md, K4 row).  **Why not fixed in CON-030 round 5:** putting variable VALUES into the context hash raises a credential question: the context hash is printed, and a truncated hash of a low-entropy secret can be guessed. | CON-294 | Bug | Queued | Low | Parent: CON-030. **What:** the guide census near-marker test (marker_ignorable / marker_norm in scripts/full_suite/run_full_suite.py) removes white space, Zs, Cf, Default_Ignorable_Code_Point and U+FFFD before it compares a line with the census markers. Every timeout reachable from install_constitution.sh, update_constitution.sh, post_update_hook.sh and jev/bootstrap.sh could hang the installer or the post-update hook (RED: T8 post_update_hook install_jev_bootstrap [uutils] rc=CAPPED). | CON-362 | Task | Queued | Low | What: without the rc_env_clean loop guard (mutant B2) the probe re-executes and leaks one descriptor per pass; at soft NOFILE 1024 exec fails after about 8 s, execfail keeps the shell and rc_env_clean returns 4, which is exactly what K8 N2 accepts. Reproduction: reproduced live by the round-13 review: --only B2, soft NOFILE 1024 2/2 'killed, but not by its property', 524288 2/2 'killed by its property'. Acceptance: K8 N2 requires exactly 2 entries into rc_env_clean besides rc=4 and a0=rc-clean-env; B2 killed by its property at both limits (N >= 3); the run records soft and hard NOFILE. Related nit: the netgate_pin.tsv line count is newline count plus one; fix in channel_census.py pin-regen. | CON-423 | Bug | In progress | High | What: the --keys-file exact-match pass of scripts/security/history_secret_scan.sh wrote every blob id into the stdin pipe of git cat-file --batch before it read any reply. With a few thousand blobs git fills its stdout pipe and stops reading stdin, the matcher fills the stdin pipe, and both wait for ever (observed on a real repository as a 2400 s timeout with 7,408 blob ids, about 300 KB). Scope: the exact-match matcher only; the gitleaks stage finishes first. Reproduction: tests/security/test_history_secret_scan_deadlock.sh builds a hermetic repository with 8000 blobs and runs the real script under timeout; on the unfixed script (sha256 e03c8098) case 1 fails with rc 124 after 60 s, and /proc wchan of both the python3 matcher and git cat-file --batch reads anon_pipe_write (docs/qa/secscan_deadlock_2026_11_16/red_wchan_prefix.txt). Fix: the ids reach git from a regular file in the work directory, and every reply is checked against the requested id (a missing or wrong reply is exit 7). Acceptance: the same test passes 17/17 within the bound, twice with identical output hashes; 14 paired mutants including the re-introduced deadlock are killed; the existing 45-case suite still passes. Evidence is gate-logic class (§11.4.289); a run with the real gitleaks binary stays owed (CON-401). | reproduced-host-fixture — REPRODUCED | root-cause-found-with-evidence |
| CON-424 | Task | Queued | Medium | What: the --keys-file exact-match pass of scripts/security/history_secret_scan.sh never searches a value shorter than 12 characters (too many chance matches in a full history). Since CON-423 this limit is documented in the script header and in docs/guides/security_hardening_scripts.md, and every run prints the note 'not searched: N shorter than 12 characters (NAMES)' with variable names and count only, also when N is 0; tests/security/test_history_secret_scan_deadlock.sh checks the 11/12 character boundary and the names and count, and paired mutants DM6, DM7 and DM8 are killed. Owed: a way to check short values (for example a context-bound pattern such as NAME=value, or an operator decision that short values are out of scope), and the decision recorded. Acceptance: either short values are covered by a tested check with its own controls, or the operator decision to leave them out is recorded and cited by the guide. | UNSET | UNSET |
| CON-425 | Bug | In progress | High | What: scripts/security/history_secret_scan.sh honours replace refs, so a repository that carries refs/replace/<secret-blob> -> <clean-blob> is scanned through the replacement. Measured by the CON-423 review round 2 (rev2_secscan_dl_ev, SHA256SUMS verified): replace_probe.txt, --exact-only, mode=plain rc=1 MYKEY=1, mode=replaced rc=0 MYKEY=0 (secret not found, clean verdict); replace_log_probe.txt: the mirror keeps 1 replace ref, git log -p --all -m sees the fake 0 times, with --no-replace-objects 1 time; rv16_probe.txt: a forced blob->tree replace makes cat-file --batch answer type tree for a blob id that --batch-check reports as blob. Pre-existing (not introduced by CON-423), source-defect, important; not fixed in the CON-423 landing. Fix idea: export GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 right after the git-env scrub so every git call (mirror, cat-file, the scanner's git log) reads the stored objects, and refuse or report replace refs. Scope: scripts/security/history_secret_scan.sh, tests/security/test_history_secret_scan.sh, tests/security/test_history_secret_scan_deadlock.sh, docs/guides/security_hardening_scripts.md. Reproduction: reproduced-host-fixture by the reviewer probes above (host-only). Acceptance: RED first with the review fixtures (replaced blob; blob->tree replace) on the current script, then GREEN with the secret found (exit 1) in both the exact-match pass and the scanner pass; paired mutant that drops the setting is killed. | reproduced-host-fixture — REPRODUCED | root-cause-found-with-evidence |

| CON-453 | Bug | Queued | High | What: gitleaks detect reads history through git log -p, and git honours diff attributes: a path marked -diff is shown as a binary change, so gitleaks never sees its added lines. Measured live with the real gitleaks 8.30.1 and git 2.53.0 on the CON-435-fixed scripts/gitleaks/gitleaks_run_scan.sh (docs/qa/gitleaks_replace_2026_11_16/probes/attributes_probe_result.txt): control repository with no attributes exit 1 with 2 leaks; the same repository with the caller's GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL naming a core.attributesFile that sets *.env -diff exit 0 PASS 0 leaks; a repository whose own committed .gitattributes holds *.env -diff, with a secret .env committed after it, exit 0 PASS 0 leaks. The committed case comes from the scanned history itself, so GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null and GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 (the CON-438 fix idea) do not cover it. Related: CON-438 (same class in scripts/security/history_secret_scan.sh, UNCONFIRMED there for the committed case; its scanner runs on a bare mirror), CON-435, CON-425. Scope: scripts/gitleaks/gitleaks_run_scan.sh and its tests; check history_secret_scan.sh too. Fix idea (UNCONFIRMED): pass a log option that forces text diffs (for example --log-opts with --text) or neutralise attributes, and confirm with the real binary. Reproduction: reproduced-live on the host by the probe above; the probe repositories are outside the repository. Acceptance: RED with the real gitleaks on both attribute cases, GREEN with the secret found, positive and negative controls, paired mutant that drops the fix killed. Source: CON-435 fixer run 2026-11-16, evidence docs/qa/gitleaks_replace_2026_11_16/. Re-minted at landing from the placeholder CON-PENDINGcon435 (section 11.4.214). | reproduced-live — REPRODUCED | not-started |
| CON-454 | Bug | Queued | Medium | What: review F1 (important, source defect, pre-existing). scripts/gitleaks/gitleaks_run_scan.sh runs gitleaks detect with the default log options; git log -p shows no diff for merge commits unless -m (or --cc/-c) is given, so a secret introduced only in the merge resolution is never scanned. scripts/security/history_secret_scan.sh already passes --log-opts="--all -m". Related: CON-435, CON-425. Scope: scripts/gitleaks/gitleaks_run_scan.sh and its tests. Reproduction: UNREPRODUCED (review finding). Acceptance: RED with the real gitleaks on a repository whose secret exists only in an evil merge, GREEN after the fix, positive and negative controls, paired mutant that drops -m killed. Source: independent review of the CON-435 vehicle (bundle 55b225f1, landed on main as merge 2ad83e18), verdict GO-with-follow-ups, evidence /mnt/DATA1TB/claude-work/scratch/rev_con435/ev/ outside the repository (SHA256SUMS sha256 56a680cb7d13c540ba086175cf1eac8470a27b98651993ca2420de26b38c04f8), gate-logic class for the fake scanner, host-only live for the real gitleaks (section 11.4.289). | UNSET | UNSET |
| CON-455 | Bug | Queued | Medium | What: review F2 (important, pre-existing). A scanned repository can carry .git/info/grafts or .git/shallow, which rewrite or cut the parent chain git log follows; GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS=1 (the CON-435 fix) disables refs/replace only, so a graft can still hide a secret commit from gitleaks detect. Fix idea (UNCONFIRMED): scan a fresh --mirror clone, which carries neither file, and report a shallow source as history-incomplete. Related: CON-437 (shallow source in history_secret_scan.sh, other script), CON-435. Scope: scripts/gitleaks/gitleaks_run_scan.sh and its tests. Reproduction: UNREPRODUCED (review finding). Acceptance: RED with the real gitleaks on a repository with a graft that hides a secret commit, GREEN after the fix, paired mutant killed. The CON-435 fixer worked around it with the one-hyphen placeholder CON-PENDINGcon435. Either add refuses an id the parser cannot read back, or the parser accepts what add writes. | CON-479 | Bug | In progress | High | What: providers/gitverse.sh read an annotated tag's commit only through GET /git/tags/{sha}, which GitVerse answers 400 VALIDATION_ERROR, so tag-get printed sha=unknown and release-ensure ended RESULT: UNVERIFIED-COMMIT (exit 5) even for the existing tag helixconstitution-v73.3; the v73.4 cut was blocked on it (operator decision 2026-11-16). Root cause (FACT, verifier defect, not a mirror lag): git ls-remote gitverse gives the same tag object e915eef7 and peeled commit aa5ae0a3 as the local tag; GET /repos/{o}/{r}/commits/{tag-object-sha} (the object.url of the matching-refs answer) answers 200 with sha aa5ae0a3, unknown objects answer 422. Fix: prov_tag_get falls back to commits/{tag-object-sha} when git/tags/{sha} is not 200, by the tag-object sha (never the tag name), and accepts only a 200 whose sha is a full commit id other than the tag-object sha. Scope: scripts/forge/providers/gitverse.sh, tests/forge/test_forge_gitverse.sh (G1-G8, G5b), tests/forge/test_forge_mutations.sh (G01-G09, M17 adapted). Acceptance: suite cases G1-G3 RED on the unfixed provider and GREEN after; every G mutant killed; live read-only tag-get reports the commit and the release-ensure dry-run for v73.3 ends OK, a wrong --sha ends FAIL tag_sha_mismatch. Evidence: docs/qa/gitverse_tag_peel_20261116/ (LIVE reads, section 11.4.289; suite gate-logic). Not proven: the GitVerse release write path (POST /releases) stays LIVE-UNPROVEN. Re-minted at landing from the vehicle's placeholder id (vehicle commit 5fc0cef9) (section 11.4.214).  Link (section 11.4.214, open original, link only; 2026-11-17, release prep): the cut-day checklist step 13 still expects GitVerse to end UNVERIFIED-COMMIT (exit 5) at the real cut (rehearsal B-M6 on helixconstitution-v73.3); recorded there as an honest gap, not a PASS. Source: v73.4 release-prep draft /mnt/DATA1TB/claude-work/scratch/relprep.GRc4vl/draft (outside the repository; SHA256SUMS sha256 c3096ff57234aff26af53012e320278e71d978077aab97cd5bbcba5f5c27f709, all entries verified OK by the lander 2026-11-17), read at main 60177435. | reproduced-live — REPRODUCED | root-cause-found-with-evidence |

The reviewer's patch idea, test_forge_gitverse_rvx.sh in the review evidence dir, kills it (reviewer_mutants_vs_extended_test.txt). Scope: tests/forge/test_forge_gitverse.sh. | CON-486 | Task | Queued | Low | What: since tag helixconstitution-v73.3 (aa5ae0a3), eight vehicle landings reached main as one squash commit each, so the reviewed head is not an ancestor of main (measured 2026-11-16 in a full clone of main 3e5e7e7d: each head is absent from main's history, acff58ae is present locally and git merge-base --is-ancestor says no). The eight: 763726e0 CON-234 rounds 1-14 (reviewed head acff58ae); f4881591 land_vehicle lander rounds 4-6 (head b8f063e1, its message records a conductor decision, option (a), not to land the orphan history); d0a6832f CON-312 (head 2e397cba); 5da39508 CON-313 (head fa1c9302); 584c287e CON-314 (head d659fddd); e90d4aea CON-401 (head e940110e); 3281d92e CON-402 (head fd799642); e5879550 CON-412 (head 380ad64a). Landings by merge commit in the same range (for example 6c743898, 2ad83e18, a6f97681, 5999ff05, f4fc7215, 66ad6efe) keep their reviewed heads and are not affected. Rules: section 11.4.288(B)(3) says a vehicle lands fast-forward-only (main advanced with merge --ff-only after the vehicle merges main into itself) and (B)(4) says its unique commits must be on main on every upstream before the vehicle is retired; section 11.4.297(A) says the landing is a fast-forward or a merge commit whose second parent is the reviewed head, never a squash or rebase merge, but it binds only a project that declared PR mode (whether this repository declared it is UNCONFIRMED). A squash keeps the reviewed tree content (for 763726e0: 390 of 397 changed paths byte-equal to acff58ae, the other 7 are the shared files its message names as integrated) but not the reviewed commits. Open operator decision, not decided here: whether the existing history stays as it is (no rewrite, section 11.4.113 forbids it in any case) and whether future landings change practice (merge or fast-forward of the reviewed head instead of squash). Scope: landing practice and the land_vehicle lander (scripts/release/land_vehicle.sh); no code change in this item. Reproduction: not applicable (process observation). Acceptance: the operator decision recorded; if practice changes, the lander and landing docs follow it. | CON-497 | Bug | In progress | Medium | What: measured 2026-11-16 in a scratch clone (evidence docs/qa/pr_delivery_decision_20261116/gate_account_free_default.txt, gate_account_free_changed_since.txt, probe_selfflag.txt). (1) Without --consumer-config the gate looks only at <root>/../config/delivery.conf (the submodule layout), so in this repository, whose declaration is config/delivery.conf inside the root, the post-update run SKIPs (exit 3). (2) With --changed-since, the consumer's own config/delivery.conf is in the corpus and is reported FAIL (it is the one file that must hold the account names). (3) With --changed-since, a changed docs/workable_items.db (binary, NUL bytes) makes the gate BLIND (exit 2). Affected: scripts/gates/cm_pr_delivery_account_name_free.sh and its post_update_hook.sh call. Acceptance: the declared config path is excluded from the corpus by path (not by content), the gate finds a root-level config/delivery.conf when the root is its own consumer, binary tracker files are handled without hiding text files; RED cases for each, paired mutants. Needs an operator decision on the exclusion mechanism (Q7 in CON-496); not fixed here. Round 2 (2026-11-16, operator decision (3) of OPD-2026-11-16-PR-DELIVERY-R2): fixed in this vehicle, pending independent review. Root cause, read in scripts/gates/cm_pr_delivery_account_name_free.sh: the default configuration was only $(dirname ROOT)/config/delivery.conf; the --changed-since loop added every changed file, including the configuration just read and a binary SQLite database, to the corpus. Fix: both <root>/config/delivery.conf and <root>/../config/delivery.conf are read by default; under --changed-since a configuration read in this run is left out by its resolved path, and a file starting with the SQLite header is left out; both are named in the output and in the verdict; a file of the fixed corpus is never left out and any other binary file stays BLIND. RED first: 5 of 8 new cases failed on the old gate (docs/qa/pr_delivery_decision_20261116/r2/red_baseline_con424.txt), 8 of 8 pass after the fix (green_con424.txt); 10 new gate mutants added to the paired mutation test. | UNSET | UNSET |
| CON-498 | Task | In progress | High | What: by the recorded operator decision OPD-2026-11-16-PR-DELIVERY-R2 (paraphrased by the conductor, not verbatim) every pull request of this repository is created, self-reviewed and merged by the agent and assigned to the main repository owner's account. The configured reviewer list (config/delivery.conf pr_reviewers) holds only that account, which is also the author, so §11.4.297(D)(2) skips it, no reviewer is possible, and the delivery reports that and ends non-zero; the platform may also refuse the agent's own review. Until answered, every delivery ends non-zero on the reviewer point and that is reported, never hidden. Acceptance: the operator's answer recorded, and config/guide (or the anchor, via its own vehicle and review) changed to match. Defects of the release tooling and tracker data, fixed class-wide in one vehicle: - D2 (section 11.4.290): CON-211, CON-235, CON-236 and CON-265 are Ready for testing with no **Verification:** provisional line, so the count line said 25 provisional while 29 items were Ready for testing; CON-190 and CON-191 have a line with no checks field. No check refused either shape. - D3: tag_and_release.sh --dry-run returned before any platform check, so a would-be SKIPPED-NO-TOKEN, an inaccessible repository or a GitHub repository-id dedup appeared only in the real run, after the tags were pushed. - D4: the dry-run plan lines of GitHub (and gitea) release legs did not say whether the pre-release flag is passed; only GitLab lines did. - D5: render_release_entry.py refused TODO/TBD but accepted DRAFT, so an entry full of DRAFT values rendered with exit 0. - D9: the template appended "." after the last ref of the unmerged-refs block, and the heading takes MANUAL:cut_date whole with no check that it is a bare date. - D10: --remotes accepted two remotes that push to one repository (the live checkout's origin pushes to gitflic; the scratch clone's origin is the same GitHub repository as github), and a repeated remote name. Acceptance (test-first, section 11.4.224): RED tests on the unfixed tools, then GREEN; paired mutants killed; a checker refuses every Ready-for-testing or In-testing item without a complete verification line, run on the real database; the six items carry complete lines written through the engine. Follow-ups of the same rehearsal: D1 (CON-379), D6 (CON-381, CON-382), D7, D8 and D11 (filed separately).  Related: CON-513 (D5, D9), CON-517 (D3, D4), CON-516 (D10 is a neighbour), CON-379 (D1), CON-381 and CON-382 (D6). | UNSET | UNSET |
